Resolves bare, as `VBA.vbObjectError`, as `VBA.Constants.vbObjectError` and in a module-level `Const`, since [#215](https://github.com/hexide-io/HexIDE/issues/215). | VB6InBoxLibraries.cs |
| `vbTab` | Supported | Member of the `VBA.Constants` module. `Chr(9)`, read from the type library and cross-checked against a compiled program's `Asc` and `Len`. Resolves bare, as `VBA.vbTab`, as `VBA.Constants.vbTab` and in a module-level `Const`, since [#215](https://github.com/hexide-io/HexIDE/issues/215). **[#1633](https://github.com/hexide-io/HexIDE/issues/1633):** rated Dies here until then, long after it resolved. | VB6InBoxLibraries.cs |
| `vbUseCompareOption` | Supported | **Not a VB6 name at all.** Widely documented as a member of `VbCompareMethod` with value -1, but the type library declares only three members (`vbBinaryCompare` 0, `vbTextCompare` 1, `vbDatabaseCompare` 2). Measured: `VbCompareMethod.vbUseCompareOption` is a compile error in vb6.exe, "Method or data member not found", and HexIDE refuses it in the same words. The bare form is an undeclared Variant, `Empty`, in both. **[#1633](https://github.com/hexide-io/HexIDE/issues/1633):** it was rated Dies, which counted a faithful refusal as a gap. | VB6InBoxLibraries.cs |
| `vbVerticalTab` | Supported | Member of the `VBA.Constants` module. `Chr(11)`, read from the type library and cross-checked against a compiled program's `Asc` and `Len`. Resolves bare, as `VBA.vbVerticalTab`, as `VBA.Constants.vbVerticalTab` and in a module-level `Const`, since [#215](https://github.com/hexide-io/HexIDE/issues/215). **[#1633](https://github.com/hexide-io/HexIDE/issues/1633):** rated Dies here until then, long after it resolved. | VB6InBoxLibraries.cs |

## Keyboard events (outside the baseline inventory)

Issue #2325 delivers TextBox keyboard events and Form KeyPreview for the measured
key codes. This is not the complete keyboard-input implementation.

| Member | Status | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| TextBox.KeyDown / KeyUp | Partial | #2325: k/K, Ctrl+A, Alt+X, Backspace, Delete, Left, F2 and modifier keys deliver KeyCode and Shift through the existing event queue, with a control-array Index where present. Other key codes retain today's behavior. Form KeyPreview interleaves form first; Form KeyCode = 0 suppresses only the control KeyDown. Nonzero KeyCode replacements are unmeasured; the control retains its original key code. | TextBoxKeyEvents.cs |
| TextBox.KeyPress / Form.KeyPreview | Partial | #2325: KeyPress precedes Change; writable KeyAscii suppresses or replaces the character in either handler. Ctrl+A delivers 1 and Backspace 8. Non-ASCII conversion is unmeasured and retains existing input behavior. | TextBoxKeyEvents.cs; VBTextBox.cs |
| TextBox.KeyPress non-ASCII replacement | Dies | #2325: ANSI conversion was not measured. A handler returning a KeyAscii outside ASCII is refused as not implemented; incoming non-ASCII text retains the existing input path. | VBTextBox.cs |
| Form, ListBox and CommandButton keyboard events | Silently wrong | #2325 remaining measured work: default/cancel/navigation arbitration, empty-form events and list/button ordering. | VBLoader.cs |
| CheckBox, OptionButton, ComboBox, scroll bars, PictureBox and UserControl keyboard events | Silently wrong | #2325 probe explicitly did not measure these controls. No keyboard behavior is inferred from TextBox; implementation and explicit not-implemented member refusals remain pending. | AttachedEvents.cs |

## StatusBar control members (outside the baseline inventory)

The baseline counts above do not enumerate common-control members. **#1087 parts A/B** implement the
MSComctlLib.StatusBar object model from [the measured oracle](vb6-fidelity-oracle/in-box-controls-the-statusbar.md)
and issuecomment-6427372779. The following gaps remain explicit refusals, rather than invented values.

| Member | Status | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `StatusBar.Panels` / `IPanels` / `IPanel` | Partial | Add, Item, Remove, Clear; default Text; fixed Width/MinWidth, property ranges and measured errors. Panel text/borders, key indicators, time/date, Simple style and measured pointer/panel events are implemented; saved panel blocks and companion picture records round-trip byte for byte. | StatusBarPanels.cs |
| `Panels.Count` | Supported | Measured Integer, including 0 after Clear; assignment raises measured 383. Panel Index is also Integer (issuecomment-6428228813). | StatusBarPanels.cs |
| `Panel.Picture` assignment / `Panels.Add(Picture)` | Partial | Saved BMP/ICO records become runtime objects; LoadPicture files and another panel picture can be assigned, or cleared with Nothing, and draw in normal style. Saved blocks remain preserved verbatim. | PanelPicture.cs, StatusBarPanels.cs, StatusBarPresentation.cs |
| Picture object members / TypeName | Dies | The recorded Type 1/3 and Width/Height 423 values have no measured return types. These members, TypeName, native handles and the remaining StdPicture surface refuse rather than invent a contract. | PanelPicture.cs, VB6BuiltIns.Inspection.cs |
| `Panel.Width` with Spring / later `Panel.Left` | Partial | Spring panels share extra width equally above their minimums, reserving 19 px for the grip; fixed panels can overflow. Hidden panels take no space. Width, MinWidth and Left are Singles. Nonempty Contents sizing remains deferred to the rest of part B; empty Contents uses MinWidth. Exact text inset/clipping remains unmeasured rendering. | StatusBarPanels.cs |
| `StatusBar.hWnd`, `MouseIcon`, `Font`, `OLEDrag`, OLE events and extender members | Dies | Native handle, DragIcon, DragMode, Negotiate, WhatsThisHelpID and unsupported object/OLE surfaces refuse; FontName/FontSize use the existing font members. Pointer events and panel events are implemented in their measured order. No hDC member is invented. | StatusBarComponentClass.cs |
| `Panel.Tag` holding another object | Partial | The measured own-bar cycle raises 35700; other object ownership is not implemented. Scalar tags work. | StatusBarPanels.cs |
| Initial lock-key state on non-Windows hosts | Partial | Key panels track presses seen by the form; querying pre-existing host toggle states is not implemented. Windows queries its keyboard state. Kana stays greyed on the measured keyboard. | StatusBarPresentation.cs |
| Other StatusBar conversions | Partial | Unmeasured enum fractions/nonnumeric input, key casing, removed-panel access, length ties/overflow, nonnumeric indices, and MousePointer/OLEDropMode assignments refuse; measured numeric ranges and half-to-even index lookup work. | StatusBarMembers.cs, StatusBarPanels.cs |

## The signature-help trap

A name the IDE describes is not a name the interpreter runs. `VbSignatures.cs` in the LSP server is
**editor metadata with no execution path**: it feeds signature help and the `vb/builtinSymbols` response
that populates the Object Browser's VBA library. The original comparison found thirteen names there
that offered a full parameter tooltip and then failed at run time:

`StrComp`, `CVar`, `CVErr`, `Shell`, `Environ`, `IIf`, `Choose`, `Switch`, `AscW`, `ChrW`, `CreateObject`,
`GetObject`, `CLDbl`.

`Shell` now runs with an explicit measured window style (#2522); its remaining measurement gaps are
listed in the inventory above.

`CLDbl` is not a VB6 function at all. `Randomize` and `Err` are listed there too, and do work: one is a statement,
the other an object. `RGB` and `QBColor` were on this list until they were implemented. Counted by comparing that
file's names with the interpreter's registry, not by hand.

Worse than a plain gap, because the IDE vouches for the call before the user makes it. Closing the gap
between those two lists — implement it, or stop advertising it — is cheap and disproportionately improves
how the IDE feels.

**A name the tooltip shows is not one VB6 takes by name, either**
([#1465](https://github.com/hexide-io/HexIDE/issues/1465)). Twenty intrinsics refuse every named argument, as a
"Syntax error", although signature help names their parameters: `Len(Expression:="abc")` does not compile (oracle,
"Named arguments"). So the interpreter keeps its own table of the names it takes, `VB6BuiltIns.NamedParameters.cs`,
measured, and `NamedParameterParityTests` holds it to `VbSignatures.cs` in one direction. Every name the interpreter
takes must be the name the tooltip shows, in the tooltip's order; the tooltip may show names VB6 refuses. Every intrinsic
the interpreter implements is in one of its tables and has a tooltip, and the same test fails when one is added without
both ([#1475](https://github.com/hexide-io/HexIDE/issues/1475)).

**Autocomplete itself is not the culprit**, contrary to an earlier draft of this section. Completion is
served from a different list (`VbKeywords.All`), and none of the thirteen appears in it. The trap is
signature help and the Object Browser, not the completion popup.

## Not yet enumerated

**TreeView (#1086):** Rows draw and accept pointer hits at the measured 16-pixel height;
`GetVisibleCount` returns complete client-row capacity as a Long, before layout and in Form_Load
as well as after Show (4 for the default-edged 80-pixel tree, 12 for the 200-pixel tree).
Saved Appearance/BorderStyle choose the measured 0/1/2/3-pixel edge, drawing and pointer origin.
MouseDown, MouseMove and MouseUp report client-area twips, subtracting that edge.
Scrolling (#2359) remains unmeasured and not implemented; label editing is implemented for visible nodes (#2360).
`TreeNodes.cs` supplies the hierarchy model and `TreeNodeAutomation.cs` now exposes
its collection and node identities to the interpreter. Injected collections can be built and walked from VB6:
Add (including named arguments), Item/default indexing, Count, Remove and Clear; node Text/default Text,
Key, Index, Children, FullPath, navigation, Parent moves, Expanded, Selected, Checked, Bold, Sorted and EnsureVisible.
Node Text accepts the measured Integer-to-string conversion; Tag reads empty and refuses Set to a control with 35700.
Count, Index and Children retain their Integer wrapping. Root/child sorting still uses the measured en-GB
word repertoire. MSComctlLib.TreeView forms now run: the control owns its Nodes collection and exposes Style,
LineStyle, Indentation (whole HIMETRIC exposed as twips), LabelEdit, HideSelection, Checkboxes, Sorted,
SelectedItem, PathSeparator and SingleSel. Deferred root sorting runs at the next dispatcher turn.
Runtime drawing now follows expansion and sibling order, Style/LineStyle, Indentation, checkboxes, Bold and HideSelection.
Mouse input now dispatches NodeClick, NodeCheck, Expand/Collapse, Click and DblClick; focus selects the first node,
and the measured keyboard navigation, Enter ordering and SingleSel sequence are bound. Expanded and EnsureVisible
raise expansion events; other code mutations stay silent. Native form persistence now preserves the measured owned-property order and values, including HIMETRIC Indentation, Font, ImageList references and OLE settings. These carried values have no designer editors; runtime bindings remain scoped as above. Remaining node members remain outstanding. No language-name inventory status or count changes yet.
Native handles remain unimplemented; no handle value is fabricated.

| TreeView member or edge | Outstanding work |
|---|---|
| Sorting outside the measured character repertoire | Not implemented: The model refuses sorting text outside the oracle word comparer’s measured character table with `NotImplementedException`, rather than using the text comparer's unmeasured fallback. Other locales remain unmeasured. |
| Remaining control members | Not implemented: HitTest, DropHighlight, FullRowSelect, HotTracking, Scroll, BorderStyle, Appearance, Enabled, Font, MousePointer, MouseIcon, OLEDragMode, OLEDropMode remain refused. SetFocus, Move and ZOrder use the shared control paths (#2557); Refresh, HitTest and OLEDrag remain refused by name with #1086; StartLabelEdit runs (#2360). Saved own properties are carried faithfully, not new runtime bindings. The unmeasured ComctlLib (5.0) TreeView remains refused. |
| Label editing and other keys | Partial (#2360): StartLabelEdit opens a shared editor in either LabelEdit mode, with cancellable BeforeLabelEdit/AfterLabelEdit and mutable NewString committed after the event returns. No selection is a no-op. Automatic mode edits an already-selected label one host double-click time after MouseUp; a double-click cancels it and manual-mode clicks never edit. Commit-before-click ordering and focus ordering for suspending handlers remain outstanding; invisible selected-node positioning and restarting an active edit are unmeasured and refuse with NotImplementedException. Keyboard dispatch covers the measured navigation keys, Enter, Space and the measured A/Q/R keys and F2 KeyDown 113; other keys keep their existing behaviour. |
| Designer preview | Not implemented: the four measured Sample Node placeholders, Font rendering and bound icons remain unavailable; the runtime tree is still used for the designer preview. |
| Error descriptions | The number-only model still supplies its existing blank descriptions. Description binding remains for the rest of A2, including the recorded cycle message; no VB6 expectations are pinned for those blanks. |
| Node Tag assignments, ForeColor and BackColor | Not implemented: Tag reads its measured empty default and Set to a control raises 35700; other Tag assignments remain unmeasured and refused. Colour binding remains, including the measured late-bound unsigned colour type. |
| ImageList binding and node images | Not implemented: Await #1083 and the runtime member binding; no image value is fabricated. Add with an image and no bound ImageList raises measured 35613. |
| Remaining automation conversions and call shapes | Control choices accept integral numeric values, Indentation accepts numeric values, flags accept Booleans and PathSeparator accepts strings. Other control conversions, fractional choices and midpoint/non-finite Indentation conversion refuse with `NotImplementedException`. Not implemented: numeric indexes (Integer, Long and Double), Integer/Long relationships, string Key/Text and Boolean flags are bound. Integer node Text assignment now uses the measured string conversion (`Text = 42`). Other conversions and unsupported argument counts refuse with `NotImplementedException`; unmeasured results and errors are not guessed. |
| Native handles (`hWnd`, `CreateDragImage`) | Not implemented: Await the native-handle decision under #1081; no handle value is fabricated. |

These preparatory binding entries are outside the language-name inventory; its counts have not moved.

The sweep behind this document did not reach everything, and a coverage document that implies otherwise is
worse than one that says where it stops. Known holes in the baseline inventory:

| Area | Missing |
|---|---|
| **The event surface** | The baseline does not list `Form_Load`, `Form_Unload`, `Form_QueryUnload`, `Form_Resize`, `Form_Paint`, `_Click`, `_Change` or `_MouseDown`. #2325 adds a separate keyboard-events table above, outside the baseline counts. |
| TabStrip (#1089) | **Partial:** an MSComctlLib.TabStrip with plain saved tabs and scalar own properties runs. Tabs.Add/Item/Count/Remove/Clear and For Each, Tab Caption/Key/Selected/ToolTipText/HighLighted and scalar Tag, SelectedItem (Set or Let), and code BeforeClick/Click are bound to the interpreter. Style, TabWidthStyle, Placement, TabStyle, MousePointer, OLEDropMode, TabFixedWidth/Height, MultiRow, MultiSelect, Separators, ShowTips and Enabled are reachable. Integer inputs and Boolean inputs only; TabMinWidth reads its measured default but its setter remains refused. The measured saved property bags round-trip; saved tab sizes load from HIMETRIC and selection starts at tab 1. Left/Right/Home/End selection and caption mnemonics are bound to window input, including focus transfer and arrow-key cancellation without Click. MouseDown/MouseUp and mouse selection are bound, including cancellation followed by Click, selected-tab/right clicks without BeforeClick, double-clicks without a second MouseDown, and Style 1 MultiSelect. The single top row now paints raised selected tabs, pressed buttons or flat buttons with optional separators, and caption mnemonic underlines; disabling does not grey the row. Toolbox remains deferred. |
| TabStrip index conversions | Nonnumeric indexes and Date indexes explicitly refuse as not implemented (#1089). Numeric indexes round half to even; strings are case-sensitive keys. Strip-property fractional, string and other unmeasured conversions, and unsupported index argument shapes, also refuse. |
| TabStrip foreign/removed tabs | Selecting a foreign or removed Tab, and reading a removed Tab's Index, explicitly refuse as not implemented (#1089); the outcomes have not been measured. |
| TabStrip unselected Tab.Selected = False | Explicitly refused as not implemented (#1089); only deselecting the selected tab was measured. |
| TabStrip DeselectAll, Style 2 | Explicitly refused as not implemented (#1089); Styles 0 and 1 were measured. |
| TabStrip remaining members | Geometry getters (Client* and Tab.Left/Top/Width/Height), Image/ImageList, Font, TabMinWidth assignment, Refresh, hWnd/MouseIcon, OLE members remain explicitly unimplemented. Geometry setters give measured 383. For Each mutation remains unmeasured and refuses, as does default assignment through a held Tab variable. Unset Tag, ToolTipText and HighLighted values are unmeasured and refused; noncircular object Tag storage awaits lifetime handling (circular tags give measured 35700). Saved plain tabs and scalar own properties load; saved ImageList/Font and tab images still refuse at F5 rather than being ignored. COMCTL32's TabStrip remains refused: measurements cover MSCOMCTL only. Top-row painting is bound; other drawing layouts remain refused. Mouse hit testing uses a provisional single top row; nondefault Placement, MultiRow, TabWidthStyle and fixed tab sizes explicitly refuse mouse input pending the layout slice; keyboard and mouse selection are bound. |
| TabStrip persistence edges | The measured save and its ImageList companion round-trip byte for byte. Unsupported property fields or values keep the form read-only and refuse serialization; runtime loading refuses them too. Extents missing from a loaded block, a saved zero-tab block and explicit empty saved Tag/ToolTipText/ImageList fields remain unmeasured and keep it read-only. Own properties and tab bags are carried internally, with no designer editor for them; designer previews tolerate saved runtime-only properties, including ImageList, Font and tab images; saved ImageList/Font and tab images remain explicitly refused at F5. The measured width rounding when the designer moves a strip remains for a later slice. |
| TabStrip drawing gaps | TabStyle 1 drawing explicitly refuses: the supplied measurements name the enum but do not describe its visual distinction. Nondefault Placement, MultiRow, TabWidthStyle, fixed sizes and row overflow also refuse drawing pending layout work. Overflow scroll arrows, images, tooltips, HighLighted painting and Font remain unimplemented; no pixel-perfect metrics or palette are claimed. The client is only a face and, for tabs, a raised frame. Controls drawn over the strip remain on the form. |
| TabStrip event binding edges | Code selection dispatches the owning form's own BeforeClick and Click, with ByRef Cancel. TabStrip control arrays and WithEvents sinks are not yet implemented; keyboard selection raises BeforeClick/Click on the owning form; KeyDown/KeyPress/KeyUp and MouseMove remain unimplemented. GotFocus and LostFocus are declared and bound; mnemonic focus acquisition raises GotFocus before BeforeClick/Click. MouseDown and MouseUp run through the owning form event queue; MouseUp sees the new selection. |
| TabStrip keyboard edges | Navigation at an endpoint or with no selection, modified Left/Right/Home/End navigation, and duplicate or multiple caption mnemonics remain unmeasured for this control; their existing behavior is unchanged. Cancelled mnemonic and Home/End selection explicitly refuse as not implemented: cancellation was measured for arrow keys only. Other drawing layouts remain for a later slice. |
| In-box classes | **#1631 part A:** placed UserControl extenders restrict members for CanGetFocus, InvisibleAtRuntime and Windowless; absent members use the existing early-bound refusal or late-bound 438, invisible Left/Top reads raise 393, and windowed hWnd is explicitly unimplemented. Parent/Object and CausesValidation/HelpContextID are recognised but unimplemented. **Part B:** tab stops respect CanGetFocus, ForwardFocus and InvisibleAtRuntime; Alt access keys forward AccessKeyPress to the receiving UserControl, and DefaultCancel supplies Default/Cancel with Enter/Esc and Ambient.DisplayAsDefault.  **Group 2A (file-save slice):** placed instances omit TabIndex when CanGetFocus=False (unless ForwardFocus=True), invisible instances save only position and existing extents, and windowless siblings save last on forms containing UserControls. An invisible instance without both recorded extents opens read-only with a save-fidelity reason; save is refused without touching the file, while dirty checks and read-only serialization remain safe. No size conversion is invented. Refused load combinations and placed UserControl designer rows remain pending. **Group 2B (PropertyPage storage slice):** HelpContextID is an editable designer property saved in the .pag; StandardSize is omitted from .pag saves. StandardSize Small/Large dimensions remain unmeasured and its designer selector is not implemented. **Part C:** ControlContainer assignment preserves coordinates and raises 425 for ineligible UserControls; ContainedControls.Count counts placed children. Align 2 docks at the bottom; BackStyle 0 is transparent. Focus and lifecycle event order follows the measured windowed/windowless/invisible cases. Initial host visibility before the form loads does not start UserControl lifecycle events (#1631 single-view regression). Other Align assignments, BackStyle reads, ContainedControls operations other than Count, and PropertyBag operations remain unimplemented. **#2062:** a .frm control without its own Font block receives a load-time copy of the form font, including inside nested Frames/PictureBoxes; explicit blocks stay intact. `FontName`/`FontSize` read that copy. Designer rendering and UserControl contents remain unmeasured. `Form`, `MDIForm`, `UserControl`, `Menu`, `VBControlExtender`, `StdFont`, `StdPicture` — the classes the enumerated members hang off. |
| RichTextLib.RichTextBox (#1100) | Registered: a minimal form can run. Text, TextRTF (also the default member), SelStart, SelLength, SelText and SelRTF use the A0 RTF model. SelBold, SelItalic, SelUnderline, SelStrikeThru, SelFontName, SelFontSize and SelColor apply to the selection; mixed character values read Null. SelAlignment applies per paragraph, reads Null for mixed paragraphs and raises 380 for the corrected invalid values, including Null. Code edits raise Change/SelChange in the measured order. Recorded Arial/Courier New TextRTF and the reduced-table `slow` SelRTF are pinned byte for byte through a running form. |
| RichTextBox remaining properties (#1100) | Explicitly not implemented: SelCharOffset, SelIndent, SelHangingIndent, SelRightIndent, SelBullet, BulletIndent, SelTabCount, SelTabs, HideSelection, DisableNoScroll, RightMargin, FileName, BackColor, Font, Hwnd, MousePointer, MouseIcon, AutoVerbMenu, OLEObjects, OLEDragMode and OLEDropMode. These refuse reads/writes with NotImplementedException, ahead of shared bindings. Native handles remain deferred. A first input/drawing slice accepts typing, Enter and Backspace, retaining character formatting and raising the measured key/selection/change order. Saved ReadOnly/MaxLength and protected runs constrain real input. Character runs draw their face, size, colour, bold, italic, underline and strikethrough; selection is black with white text and hides on focus loss by default. Click and right-click raise MouseDown, MouseUp and Click before caret selection; double-click selects the word and its trailing space after DblClick. The measured Select All, tab insertion, inert bold/underline and paragraph alignment shortcuts work, as do plain-text copy/paste and Undo with document formatting retained. Caret scrolling also offsets mouse hit-testing. Shift-arrow, Shift+Home/End and command-arrow selection now follow the measured endpoints (issuecomment-6444900026): CRLF steps, the hidden final break, active-end selection, collapse and punctuation word stops. Wrapped vertical endpoints, lone-break navigation and non-ASCII word endpoints explicitly refuse; command-left collapse of a selection and collapsing a hidden-break selection remain refused as unmeasured; without a selection, plain Left/Right/Home/End/Up/Down use the recorded Shift endpoints. Lone-break navigation and wrapped Home/End/Up/Down refuse; with a selection, plain Home/End/Up/Down refuse their unmeasured endpoints. Modified mouse clicks, drag selection and word selection outside the recorded ASCII-word-with-trailing-space shape explicitly refuse as unmeasured. Formatted clipboard copying, paste sources carrying non-text formats and Undo after code edits explicitly refuse; paragraph alignment/indent/bullet/tab drawing, RightMargin, character offsets remain deferred. Saved BackColor, Appearance, BorderStyle and Enabled now drive box drawing: disabled text uses the grey-text brush and the background uses button face; re-enabling restores the saved colour. BorderStyle 0 omits the border; Appearance selects flat or inset edges. Enabled reads/writes Boolean values; Appearance and BorderStyle read saved/default values and refuse runtime writes with 383. BackColor runtime access still refuses, including its unsigned colour getter. Saved BackColor accepts hexadecimal and decimal OLE_COLOR spellings, including the VB6 save’s 12648447. Invalid spellings still refuse conversion. Designer instances draw the existing labelled, view-only placeholder, including for unsupported RTF. Keyboard event codes currently cover letters, digits, Space, Enter, Backspace and Tab; other keys and ByRef cancellation remain unimplemented. Non-ASCII KeyPress conversion is unmeasured and explicitly refuses before editing; code-driven ANSI text remains supported. Unfocused selection drawing with HideSelection False is unmeasured and remains unimplemented (no selection drawn); runtime HideSelection access continues to refuse. Saved TextRTF now loads into the model, with MultiLine False keeping the first paragraph on load and Text assignment. Other saved own properties (including ReadOnly, the persisted spelling of Locked) are carried without exposing designer editors; runtime access still refuses except Enabled, Appearance, BorderStyle, Locked, MaxLength and read-only MultiLine/ScrollBars. RichTextLib is available in Components/the toolbox; placement seeds the name as RTF and declares RICHTX32.OCX. The measured saved .frm/.frx round-trips byte for byte, with recomputed extents, fixed own-property order, Enabled/TextRTF always written and inline TextRTF moved to a length-prefixed companion record. Unsupported inline RTF normalization holds the designer read-only. Locked and SelProtected now expose Boolean runtime access and constrain real input without preventing code edits; mixed protection reads Null. MultiLine and ScrollBars read the saved/default values and reject runtime writes with 383. MaxLength reads the saved/default limit and accepts the recorded 0, 5 and 20 assignments; Text is truncated to the limit, and SelText appended at a full limit is dropped. Other limit assignments, reducing the limit below existing text, code selection replacement away from a full end, and TextRTF/SelRTF edits with a nonzero limit explicitly refuse pending measurements; single-line TextRTF and selection paragraph insertion also refuse their unmeasured conversions. An empty first paragraph in saved single-line RTF refuses until its output shape is measured. The RichTextLib registry description and unsized default extents are unmeasured: the dialog labels the library by its measured name, and the unused DefaultExtent member refuses. |
| RichTextBox.SelColor scalar getter (#1100) | Not implemented: the oracle records late-bound colour reads as unsigned Long Variants, VarType 19, which Vb6Value does not represent. A non-Null SelColor read refuses with NotImplementedException instead of returning subtype 3. Mixed colours still read Null; assignments apply to the model and write the measured RTF colour table. The negative/system-colour coercions and retained 16777216 are covered at the model boundary. |
| RichTextBox remaining methods (#1100) | Find, Span, UpTo, GetLineFromChar, LoadFile, SaveFile, SelPrint, Refresh and OLEDrag explicitly refuse with NotImplementedException. |
| RichTextBox RTF limits and conversions (#1100) | A0 retains its measured font/colour tables, character runs, paragraph alignment/indents/tab stops and Symbol bullet groups. Unknown RTF words/destinations, mixed-format or paragraph SelRTF output, selection font charset normalisation, lone CR/LF plain-Text output, multiple bullet paragraphs and other bullet/table syntax remain refused. SelRTF fragments without a font table refuse: the blue-fragment probe records text and colour, but not the implicit insertion formatting needed for a faithful RTF round trip. Complete measured single-paragraph character-format selection documents are accepted. Paragraph SelRTF insertion and bullet-font remapping refuse. RTF output for pending caret insertion formatting refuses rather than losing that formatting. RTF output for newly assigned faces other than the recorded MS Sans Serif/Courier New entries refuses while SelFontName remains readable; incoming font-table entries retain their metadata. Font names needing non-ASCII encoding or escaping also refuse output. Empty SelFontName restoration has no measured general rule and refuses. Unmeasured non-String text assignments, String/Null numeric conversions, fractional integer conversions and non-Boolean flag conversions refuse; the measured SelBold Null/5 and SelAlignment Null cases are handled explicitly. Fractional SelFontSize conversion outside half points refuses; 2000 reads 1638.35 but its unmeasured RTF conversion refuses on output, as does a SelColor beyond the RTF RGB range (the measured 16777216 remains stored in the model). Other unmeasured upper-size conversions refuse. |
| Extender methods | **#1631 part A:** UserControl SetFocus is absent with CanGetFocus False or InvisibleAtRuntime True; part B supplies focusable tab stops and access-key forwarding; ForwardFocus also removes SetFocus and TabStop while retaining TabIndex. **#852:** a form's Left/Top read its outer position as Singles in twips whatever ScaleMode; assignments and Move move the window and preserve assigned whole twips. CenterScreen applies at Show. `Move`, `ZOrder`, `SetFocus`, `Refresh`, `Show`, `Hide`, `PopupMenu`, `Arrange`, `ShowWhatsThis`. Not enumerated, though `Move` on a control runs since #1071 (oracle, "Move, ScaleWidth and ScaleHeight") and on a form since #1569 (oracle, "A form's Move"), `SetFocus` since #1061 (oracle, "SetFocus"), and `ZOrder` on a control since #1503 (oracle, "What ZOrder does at run time"). |
| Graphics methods | `Point`, `PaintPicture`, `TextWidth`, `TextHeight`, `ScaleX`, `ScaleY` (and `Line` appears only as a statement, not in its graphics form). |
| Printer methods | Every `Printer` row is a *property*; `Printer.Print`, `.NewPage`, `.EndDoc`, `.KillDoc`, `.Line` are absent. |
| Drag and drop | `Drag` and `OLEDrag` — the methods the enumerated drag constants are arguments to. |
| `Open` clauses | The compound lexer tokens `Lock Read`, `Lock Write`, `Lock Read Write`, `Read Write`. |

The events gap is the largest and the most consequential: event handlers are how a VB6 form program is
structured, so a coverage document that omits them is silent about the most-used surface in the language.

## Maintenance

This document was generated from a full sweep of the VB6 surface and then hand-edited. When a status
changes, edit the row. When adding a construct, add its row in the same change — a coverage document that
drifts is worse than none, because it is quoted rather than checked.

Counts are checked by `MissingLanguageDocTests` and recounted from the rows, never edited by hand.
Percentages are rounded to the nearest whole number.

**Source citations name a file and never a line number.** That is deliberate. Line numbers here have rotted
twice: `interpreter-gaps.md`'s own audit found roughly nineteen of thirty pointing past end-of-file, and
this document's first draft had every `StatementExecutor.cs` citation off by 29 lines because a commit
moved underneath it between the sweep and the commit. The **quoted throw message** in the Detail column is
the anchor — it is greppable and it moves with the code. Please do not add line numbers back.

Two known drift hazards, both live:

1. [`interpreter-gaps.md`](interpreter-gaps.md) has its own `Partial` section describing many of the same
   constructs. It owns *classification*; this file owns *coverage*. Do not restate its reasoning here —
   link to it.
2. `IDE/HexIDE.Runtime/README.md` carries a walls table that has already gone stale at least once
   (it listed `Implements` as permanently out of scope after it had been implemented).

### MSComctlLib.Toolbar — runtime control model (#1088)

This staged control implementation is separate from the 123 in-box object names counted above. Measurements:
[the Toolbar oracle](vb6-fidelity-oracle/in-box-controls-the-toolbar.md), issuecomment-6428845889.

| Surface | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Empty Toolbar scalar properties | Partial | Runtime MSComctlLib registration for top-level bars; measured defaults, Style/TextAlignment validation, and no-effect ButtonWidth/ButtonHeight/Height writes. Runtime captions, normal/check/group value states, separators, placeholder space and dropdown arrows draw with host-theme sizing; flat Style and ShowTips update the visuals. File Align 1/2 is retained; no toolbox entry yet, and assigning Align is refused pending alignment support. Assignments accept the measured scalar types. Other conversions and unmeasured enum values refuse: Appearance supports only 1, MousePointer and OLEDropMode only 0, and BorderStyle only 0/1 in this slice. |
| Buttons, IButton, ButtonMenus, IButtonMenu | Partial | Runtime Add, Item, Remove, Clear, Count; item scalar defaults, measured writes/errors, default Caption/Text and group Value changes. Designed top-level bars load the measured Buttons/ButtonMenus property bags, including numeric ImageIndex values, Object.* aliases and twips placeholder widths. Nested bars and control-array bars retain the existing load refusal. |
| Toolbar item geometry and populated ButtonWidth/ButtonHeight | Partial | #2373: shared width follows the widest one-line caption in the host message font, using GDI whole-string extents on Windows and per-glyph advances rounded to whole pixels elsewhere (#2334) (+7/minimum 23 px below, +27/minimum 43 px right). Ampersands count in measurement; tabs/LF/CR add 8/8/1 px and are not drawn. Caption changes, insertion/removal and visibility recompute layout; Clear retains dimensions. Captioned buttons are 38 px high below with 16 px images (also without a list), and 54 px with 32 px images; right-aligned captions with 16 px images remain 22 px high. Bound 32 px images impose a 39 px minimum width, while caption widths retain #2373's rule. Wrappable bars use additional rows; nonwrappable bars overflow. Flat bars start at Top 0 with no extra height; standard bars start at 2 px and add 4 px. Separators are 8 px, flat dropdowns add 15 px (standard bars retain the earlier measured 13 px arrow), placeholders use their assigned width. The oracle's Tabx/Twolines widths exceed the rule by one pixel; tests preserve the discrepancy. |
| Unmeasured Toolbar layout | Dies | Non-default-style item Top/Height remain refused. Hidden Left/Top refuse rather than return VB6's native/p-code-dependent junk; hidden Width/Height remain zero. Image sizes other than square 16/32 px, 32 px images with captions right, and 32 px images without captions remain refused pending measurements. |
| Toolbar item conversions outside measured scalar types | Dies | Refused rather than guessed, including object Tags (except the measured Toolbar assignment error 35700). Image selector conversions outside Integer/Long and exact keys still refuse (#2334). |
| Toolbar ImageList and button Image (#2334) | Partial | Runtime binding to a populated ImageList; index/key Image writes and Add validate against ListImages, retaining Integer/String selectors. Invalid indexes/keys preserve the old value. Unbinding or replacing a bound list clears every Image to Integer 0; replacement resizes the buttons immediately. Binding an empty list raises 35613. Bound ListImages can grow, but Remove/Clear raise 35617 until unbound. Saved top-level bars bind their named populated ImageList after controls are created, preserving saved Integer image indexes and the form/companion bytes. Bound images paint at the measured offsets (centred/3 px down below, 5/3 px right), retaining empty image space. Paint reads the list’s current UseMaskColor. Populated hot/disabled lists select by the normal image’s position, with hot images only on flat bars and missing alternate indexes drawing nothing. DisabledImageList images paint at full opacity while the disabled caption retains its existing theme pixels ([#2490, issuecomment-6462915346](https://github.com/hexide-io/HexIDE/issues/2490#issuecomment-6462915346)); disabled buttons without an alternate entry still draw no image. |
| Remaining Toolbar image cases (#2334) | Dies | Negative/empty/coerced Image selectors, key-case variants, missing/ambiguous/wrong-type saved list references and other binding value types refuse pending measurements. Alternate-list assignments outside populated ImageList objects (including clearing/empty lists and alternate geometries beyond the measured 16 px lists), and disabled/hot rendering without an alternate list or with mixed normal/alternate geometry remain unmeasured (retaining no-image rendering). The alternate lists’ measured defaults read Nothing. |
| Controls, MouseIcon, hWnd | Dies | Refused by member name; native handles follow #1081. |
| Toolbar pointer and menu events | Partial | MouseDown, MouseUp, Click then ButtonClick; check/group Value updated before ButtonClick, with no ButtonClick for the pressed group button. Double-click's second press raises ButtonClick alone. Separators, placeholders, disabled buttons, empty space and right-clicks raise only the generic mouse/click sequence. Dropdown arrow raises MouseDown then ButtonDropDown; visible menu items support pointer/keyboard activation with ButtonMenuClick alone. MouseMove, Change/customization and OLE drag events remain unimplemented; their interaction behavior was not measured. |
| Refresh, Customize, SaveToolbar, RestoreToolbar, OLEDrag | Dies | Refused by member name. Customization/property pages and Change are unmeasured. The saved Toolbar form and its ImageList companion round-trip byte for byte through verbatim preservation (part C); designed top-level bars now load at run time. Editing and rewriting their property bags in the designer remains unfinished: all Toolbar scalar properties and Buttons are view-only, with carried placeholders excluded from the Properties component list. The C3 headless regression edits the surrounding form through Properties, saves, reloads and reads the edit back while preserving both Toolbar blocks and the companion byte for byte. |

### TabDlg.SSTab — runtime control model (#1090)

SSTab is registered as a runtime container. Saved blocks load their page lists and flags; programs can switch
their pages, change captions and tab counts, use indexed enabled/visible flags, and observe the
position-based page engine and TabStop changes. Click reports PreviousTab after movement. The inert
default member reads zero and ignores Let assignments. The runtime now draws the basic tab strip and page,
including rows, active captions, Style, orientation, wrapping and focus indication; drawing does not inset
the child host or change the measured position-based page engine. Appearance/picture binding and the saved-state
designer preview remain for later slices. Mouse captions, focused arrow/Ctrl+Tab navigation and Alt caption
mnemonics now switch pages, with measured mouse ordering and focus. MouseDown completes with the old Tab
before the queued page switch and Click, including when the interpreter yields (#2358). The in-box language inventory counts above
are unchanged: external-control members are tracked separately here.

| Member | Status | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| `TabDlg.SSTab.Tabs` (zero or negative, empty control) | **Dies** | The oracle did not separate the empty-control range from refusal for recorded children. Explicitly not implemented; no VB6 value is guessed. |
| SSTab numeric, Boolean and caption coercions | **Dies** | Only numeric values (whole numbers for integer properties), actual Booleans and actual Strings are accepted. Null, string-to-number, fractional integer and overflow conversions are unmeasured and explicitly refused. Saved scalar coercions also refuse beyond integers, 0/-1 Boolean flags and String captions. |
| SSTab `Picture`, `TabPicture`, `BackColor`, `ForeColor`, `Font`, `MousePointer`, `MouseIcon`, `OLEDropMode` | **Dies** | Declared members explicitly refuse as not implemented pending the remaining object-model slice. Bad TabPicture indexes still raise measured error 381. |
| SSTab `hWnd`, `OLEDrag`, `AboutBox` | **Dies** | Declared members explicitly refuse as not implemented; no native handle is fabricated. |
| SSTab explicit `[_Tab]` spelling | **Won't load** | The existing grammar rejects bracketed identifiers beginning with underscore. The implicit default member works; explicit spelling is left for grammar work. |
| SSTab `TabHeight` / `TabMaxWidth` in non-twip containers | **Dies** | The pixel sample reads 33 for 500 twips; generic control geometry would return a fractional pixel value. The full conversion/rounding rule is not recorded, so these dimensions explicitly refuse outside twips. |
| SSTab missing saved extent basis | **Dies** | Default extents for a loaded instance without extents are unmeasured; writing that case explicitly refuses rather than borrowing the double-clicked designer size. |
| SSTab saved own properties and page lists | **Partial** | Loads counts, captions, style/orientation, HIMETRIC dimensions, WordWrap/ShowFocusRect and page lists. ControlEnabled suppresses listed TabStops; missing/true tab-zero flags reproduce the measured load quirk. Saved TabEnabled/TabVisible and Control(i).Enabled do not alter runtime flags. Positions are trusted. Appearance, pictures and drag members remain explicitly unimplemented as above; the writer carries these saved values and companion records in measured order, always emitting TabHeight in HIMETRIC. Both saved corpus forms round-trip exactly. Forms stay read-only until designer edits can maintain page membership. |
| SSTab saved HIMETRIC rounding beyond recorded samples | **Dies** | Only 0, 520 → 295 twips, 900 → 510 and 1500 → 850 are recorded. Other saved dimensions explicitly refuse pending a measured conversion/rounding rule. |
| SSTab saved references to array elements or unresolved children | **Dies** | Only measured plain child names resolve. Unresolved references explicitly refuse rather than guessing membership. |

### MSComDlg.CommonDialog — runtime control model (#1084)

These require MSComDlg and are outside the intrinsic-name counts above. The scalar object-model slice implements
MaxFileSize, Copies, FromPage, ToPage, Min, Max, Orientation, HelpCommand, HelpFile, HelpKey, PrinterDefault,
FontUnderline and FontStrikethru, with the measured defaults and assignment checks. FilterIndex rejects negative
values; FileTitle and hDC reject writes with 383. See the oracle's
[in-box CommonDialog measurements](vb6-fidelity-oracle/in-box-controls-the-commondialog.md).

| Member | Status | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| `CommonDialog` saved properties | Supported | The VB6-saved four-control form round-trips byte for byte in `corpus/inbox-controls/saved/commondialog/`. Own lines keep their measured order and individual names (no Font block); explicit defaults are omitted. Missing lines retain the measured runtime defaults; HelpContext runtime assignment remains refused as listed below. Existing designer insertion places a new control before its peers. |
| `CommonDialog` single-file dialog results | Partial | Open adds PathMustExist for FileMustExist even on Cancel; picks report ExtensionDifferent, complete DefaultExt (or a simple selected Save filter extension), and Open rejects a path longer than MaxFileSize with 20476. CancelError remains 32755 and leaves file properties alone. Save buffer errors, exact buffer-fit boundaries, extension casing and resetting an already-set ExtensionDifferent bit are unmeasured and retain previous behavior. |
| `CommonDialog.ShowOpen` multi-select | Partial | AllowMultiselect enables multiple picks through both hosts. Explorer returns the folder without a trailing separator followed by NUL-separated names; the old format returns a trailing folder separator and space-separated names. FileTitle is empty for several picks; an Explorer single pick retains the plain path, title and measured ExtensionDifferent flag. |
| `CommonDialog.ShowOpen` unmeasured multi-select shapes | Dies | Non-local additional selections in the standalone host, selections spanning folders, and old-format names or folders containing spaces are refused as not implemented: their VB6 encodings are unmeasured. Native old-dialog appearance and its uppercase CurDir side effect remain unavailable; this slice implements the measured return strings only. |
| `CommonDialog.ShowSave` extension from an unavailable filter | Dies | Refused as not implemented when a name needs an extension but the host cannot identify the selected filter among several. Non-simple filter patterns are also refused on this path: their extension-completion rules are unmeasured. |
| `CommonDialog.hDC` read | Partial | After accepted ShowPrinter with Flags 0, reads the measured Long 0. Before acceptance, the measured initial nonzero printer handle has no native counterpart and is refused as not implemented. |
| `CommonDialog.Action` | Partial | Explicit reads raise 394; a Let of the control reads this write-only default member and raises 394 too. Integer/Long Action 1–6 dispatches to ShowOpen, ShowSave, ShowColor, ShowFont, ShowPrinter and ShowHelp respectively, awaiting each method and retaining its CancelError behavior; measured invalid writes 0, 7, 8 and -1 raise 380. |
| `CommonDialog.Action` unmeasured writes | Dies | Other unmeasured values and assignment conversions remain refused. Action 3 and 5 reach the managed colour and print dialogs (#2299). Implicit default-member writes retain their previous behavior. |
| `CommonDialog.ShowHelp` | Partial | Returns without error for measured HelpCommand 0, 2 and 3, with or without HelpFile, including through Action 6. Other commands are refused as not implemented. No external help application is launched. |
| `CommonDialog.ShowPrinter` | Partial | #2299: managed dialog, also through Action 5. Flags 0 with default page range/orientation and Copies 1 or 3 accepts with Copies 1, Orientation 1 and hDC 0; Cancel preserves properties and raises 32755 under CancelError. No printing takes place. |
| `CommonDialog.ShowPrinter` unmeasured configurations | Dies | Other flags (including native cdlPDReturnDC), non-default page ranges/orientation and Copies other than 1/3 refuse as not implemented. Host dialogs outside the IDE require an application callback, as for ShowFont/ShowColor. |
| `CommonDialog.ShowFont` effects | Partial | Font name, size, bold and italic use the existing host dialog, including with Flags 0. Underline, strikeout and Color writeback remain outstanding; existing behavior is unchanged. |
| `CommonDialog.HelpContext` | Dies | Still refused as not implemented; only its default is measured, not its assignment conversion. |
| `CommonDialog` control arrays / contained controls | Dies | Remain refused as not implemented; registration does not extend the measured scalar control to these unmeasured cases. |
| `CommonDialog.Color` numeric conversions | Dies | Unsigned Automation Long reads have VarType 19 and preserve the measured value (including `&H80000005`); numeric use and declared-scalar conversion are refused pending measurements. Variant storage and VarType work. TypeName and IsNumeric retain their previous behavior; their CommonDialog results are unmeasured. |

Runtime Left/Top reads raise 393 and writes raise 382; reads of Visible, Width, Height, Enabled and hWnd raise 438.
FontName ignores a missing face and keeps an accepted name's spelling, using the existing cross-platform font-name
policy. Empty FontName assignments, unavailable faces loaded from files, and unmeasured extender assignments and
conversions retain their previous behavior; VarType/Is* default-member inspection is also unchanged. Library/component
registration now uses the MSComDlg library record and a component class, without a loader text-block special case.
Color reads carry VarType 19 and the measured unsigned value; assigning integral -1 raises 6. Unmeasured
assignment conversions retain the previous coercion. Dialog return semantics are part B;
file persistence is part C (now measured).

## MaskedEdBox foundation (outside the baseline inventory)

The #1098 first slice uses [the measured MaskedEdBox oracle](vb6-fidelity-oracle/the-maskededbox-mask-text-and-keystrokes.md).
The model is bound to MSMask.MaskEdBox for code-assigned text and selection reads. Keyboard insert/removal and navigation now bind to the model. The measured `##-##` SelText writes insert, drop refused characters, truncate overflow and reset SelStart to zero. Clipboard copy/cut honors ClipMode, and paste inserts, skips refused characters and cuts overflow in the measured rows; the measured saved form now round-trips, including duplicate OLE modes, an always-written PromptChar and the omission of Text. Saved own properties remain carried rather than runtime bindings.

| Surface | Status | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| `MSMask` library constants | Supported | The measured library GUID/version and all 66 enum constants resolve through the existing referenced-library machinery. The table is derived from MSMASK32-constants.jsonl; enum qualification stays strict. |
| `MaskEdBox` text model | Partial | Runtime component and interpreter binding: ASCII mask characters, Text assignments with PromptInclude on/off, ClipText, unformatted FormattedText, selection reads, ClipMode, measured 380s and Change on refused Text assignment. Default Text reads, code-set selection and the measured `##-##` SelText writes are bound; clipboard copy/cut uses ClipMode, and paste implements all ten measured rows (including a Change on wholly refused input and the single ValidationError on overflow); ASCII typing inserts up to the first prompt, Backspace and Delete pull across literals and close empty places, and refused input dispatches ValidationError. KeyDown/KeyPress/KeyUp follow the measured removal ordering; plain arrows/Home/End retain literal caret positions; the displayed prompts are independent of PromptInclude. |
| `MaskEdBox` unfinished members | Dies | Runtime creation now works for a box without saved own properties. Format, Refresh/OLEDrag, default Text writes, focus/AutoTab effects, measured drawing, toolbox placement, own-property designer editing and runtime loading of saved own properties remain for subsequent slices. Persistence now normalizes the measured input to VB6’s save and preserves an unedited save; saved own properties still refuse explicitly at F5 and in the designer preview. MouseIcon persistence remains unmeasured and refuses saving, keeping that form read-only. The internal model also refuses replacing a mask with nonempty Text, changing PromptChar, non-ASCII input, signs assigned to a `9` place, negative MaxLength, unmeasured ClipMode values, unknown mask characters, clearing a mask and a trailing escape. |
| `MaskEdBox` unmeasured surfaces | Dies | KeyCode/KeyAscii handler modifications and modified keyboard shortcuts other than clipboard gestures and navigation with an active selection refuse pending measurement/implementation. Native hWnd, HideSelection's visual effect, drag/drop and assignment conversions refuse through the runtime member table. Non-underscore prompt typing with AllowPrompt remains unavailable. SelText writes outside the measured `##-##` mask, starting on its literal, with prompt characters or non-ASCII text refuse; no-mask selection writes remain unmeasured. Clipboard selection outside `##-##`, an empty clipboard selection, cutting from the literal, paste for other masks, starting on the literal, non-text clipboards, prompt/non-ASCII clipboard text, AutoTab paste, and no-mask paste with a selection or MaxLength overflow refuse pending measurement/implementation. The existing model treats separator characters literally; their locale-sensitive behaviour remains unmeasured and unchanged. MaxLength readings for masks with case directives or escapes are not pinned and need measurement. Those MaxLength reads refuse, as do Long-to-Integer MaxLength narrowing and readings beyond Integer storage; the other measured members bind independently. |
